- **layer.es**: Cómo funciona CAPA (LAYER, alias CA) de AutoCAD en español: Administrador de propiedades de capas, crear, suprimir, desactivar y bloquear. Pruébalo gratis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.es.md))
- **layer.fr**: CALQUE (LAYER, alias PL) d'AutoCAD en français : le Gestionnaire des propriétés des calques, créer, supprimer, désactiver et verrouiller. Essayez-le en ligne. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.fr.md))
- **layer.hu**: Fóliák az AutoCAD-ben: a FÓLIA (LAYER, FO) parancs és a Fóliatulajdonság-kezelő oszlopai, új fólia, törlés, aktuálissá tétel, kikapcsolás és zárolás. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.hu.md))
- **layer.it**: Come funziona LAYER (LA) di AutoCAD in italiano: le colonne del Gestore proprietà layer, Nuovo, Elimina e Imposta corrente, e come agiscono On e Bloccato. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.it.md))
- **layer.ja**: AutoCAD の LAYER[画層管理](LA)の使い方を解説します。画層プロパティ管理の各列、新規作成・削除・現在に設定、表示とロックがオブジェクトに与える影響。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.ja.md))
- **layer.ko**: AutoCAD 도면층 특성(LAYER, LA), 캐드 레이어 사용법: 도면층 특성 관리자의 열, 새 도면층, 삭제, 현재로 설정, 켜기와 잠금이 객체에 주는 영향. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.ko.md))
- **layer.pl**: Jak pracować z warstwami w AutoCADzie: polecenie WARSTWA (LAYER, skrót WA), Menedżer właściwości warstw, tworzenie, usuwanie, wyłączanie i blokowanie. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.pl.md))
- **layer.pt-BR**: O LAYER do AutoCAD em português, CAMADA (alias CA): Gerenciador de propriedades de camadas, criar, excluir, desativar e bloquear layers. Experimente grátis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.pt-BR.md))
- **layer.ru**: Как работать со слоями в AutoCAD: команда СЛОЙ (LAYER), столбцы диспетчера свойств слоев, создание, удаление, текущий слой, отключение и блокировка. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/layer.ru.md))
- **layer.set-by-pattern**: Move every entity whose current layer matches a regex onto a target layer. Creates target layer if missing. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/autocad-2026/commands/layer.set-by-pattern.md) · ★7)
- **layer.set-by-pattern**: Move every element whose **current layer name matches a regex** onto a **target layer** (which should already exist — see the layer-creation gotcha). Returns the target layer id, the number of elements moved, and whether the target had to be created — the bulk "re-home everything on `*_TEMP` onto `AR_WALL`" primitive. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/allplan-2025/commands/layer.set-by-pattern.md) · ★7)
- **layer.set-by-pattern**: Move every object whose current layer matches a regex onto a target layer. Used for cleaning imported models (e.g., "anything starting with `_IFC_` → layer `IFC-IMPORTS`"). Creates the target layer if it doesn't exist. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/rhino-8/commands/layer.set-by-pattern.md) · ★7)

- **lazy-core.checkup**: Run when the operator asks for one pass over everything — 'check my whole config', 'run all the audits', 'is anything broken across the lazycortex plugins' — and wants to be asked at the end what to fix. Sibling `/lazy-core.audit` only measures and never fixes; `/lazy-core.doctor` is one cross-artifact scan with a per-finding fix loop. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/commands/lazy-core.checkup.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-core.help**: Run when the operator asks what lazycortex-core can do, which of its verbs handles a job, or what is available now that it is installed — config install / audit / doctor / setup, the expert job runtime and routines, model and provider routing, git staging-lock verbs, guard scans, and the change-history log agents. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/commands/lazy-core.help.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-dev**: Chains multiple `/improve` loops in a safety-ordered sequence. Drives all underperforming metrics toward their targets in one session or generates an overnight script. ([drshailesh88/build_playbook](https://github.com/drshailesh88/build_playbook/blob/HEAD/commands/lazy-dev.md))
- **lazy-diagram.help**: Run when the operator asks what lazycortex-diagram can do, which verb draws a new picture versus repairing an existing fence, or what kinds and formats are on offer — lists the diagram engine's surface: draw / fix / audit / install, the mermaid and ASCII drawer agents, and the authoring rule governing templates and style schemes. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-diagram/commands/lazy-diagram.help.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-experts.help**: Run when the operator asks what lazycortex-experts ships, which generic expert fits a piece of work, or how to assemble a named specialist from persona agents and aspects in `lazy.settings.json[experts]`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-experts/commands/lazy-experts.help.md) · ★1)
- **lazy-handoff**: Produce a factual resumable handoff from current LazyTrae state. ([elvinzhao10/LazyTrae](https://github.com/elvinzhao10/LazyTrae/blob/HEAD/lazytrae-plugin/.trae/commands/lazy-handoff.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
